{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-5-5","id":"msg_011CgR5fZNdcs76WLPQ7yRt3","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"=== BEGIN ARTIFACT ARCHITECTURE.md ===\n# ARCHITECTURE: DrydockStats\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261026 V1 |\n| Description | Package layout, boundaries and pipeline of the ddkstat command-line reporter for Drydock's LLM execution log. |\n| Depends On  | None |\n| Provides    | go.mod, internal/, cmd/ddkstat/, bin/ |\n| Consumes    | None |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nddkstat reads a JSON Lines log (`logs/llm.jsonl`) and writes one of six CSV reports to standard output. It is a single-process, single-threaded CLI. It has no database, no network access and no configuration files. It never writes to the log it reads.\n\n## Technology Stack\n\nDerived from `TECHNOLOGY_STACK.md`:\n\n| Technology | Where it applies |\n|---|---|\n| Go 1.22 or newer, standard library only | All application code under `cmd/` and `internal/`. |\n| go test and go vet | Test runner behind `bin/test.sh`; tests live beside the code in `*_test.go`. |\n| JSON Lines | Input format, decoded with `encoding/json`, one object per physical line. |\n| POSIX sh | `bin/test.sh` and the supplied grader `sources/run.sh`. |\n\nNo third-party module is permitted. `go.mod` declares the module `ddkstat`, a `go` directive of 1.22 or newer, and no `require` lines.\n\n## Layout\n\n```text\ngo.mod                    module ddkstat\ncmd/ddkstat/main.go       package main; the only caller of os.Exit\ninternal/logread/         read, validate, dedupe and normalize log records\ninternal/filter/          pure record filters (target, date range)\ninternal/format/          minutes and cost text formatting\ninternal/report/          one aggregate-and-write function per report\ninternal/cli/             Run, argument parsing, report registry\nbin/test.sh               go vet ./... then go test ./...\nsources/                  read-only scoring assets; never touched by application code\n```\n\n## Module Ownership\n\n| Module | Owns | May access |\n|---|---|---|\n| `internal/logread` | Opening-agnostic reading of an `io.Reader`, line classification, skip and duplicate rules, numeric normalization, model attribution | `encoding/json`, `bufio`, `io`, `strings` |\n| `internal/filter` | Target and date-range predicates over `[]logread.Record` | `logread` types only |\n| `internal/format` | `Minutes` and `Cost` text | `fmt` |\n| `internal/report` | Aggregation and CSV writing for each report | `logread`, `format`, `io` |\n| `internal/cli` | Argument parsing, exit-code mapping, opening the log file read-only, report registry | `os`, `flag`, `time`, all internal packages |\n| `cmd/ddkstat` | Process start and `os.Exit` | `internal/cli` |\n\nPersistence, configuration files, file stores and external services: none. The log file is opened read-only by `internal/cli` and passed to `logread` as an `io.Reader`.\n\n## Pipeline\n\nParse arguments, read the log, drop skipped and duplicate lines, filter, aggregate, format. Parsing, aggregating and formatting are separate functions, each unit-tested.\n\n## Shared Types and Interfaces\n\n`logread.Read(r io.Reader, warn io.Writer) (Result, error)` returns `Result{Records []Record, Skipped int}`. The error is non-nil only when reading from `r` fails.\n\n`logread.Record` carries: `ExecutionID`, `StartedAt`, `Date` (first 10 characters of `StartedAt`), `Status`, `Failed`, `Command`, `Target`, `Provider`, `Model`, `InputTokens`, `CachedTokens`, `OutputTokens` (all `int64`), `CostUSD` (`float64`), `ElapsedMS` (`int64`), `ReturnCode` (`*int`, nil when null or absent) and `TimedOut` (`bool`).\n\n`cli.Run(args []string, stdout, stderr io.Writer) int` is the single entry point; exit codes are 0 success, 1 unreadable log, 2 usage error. Report writers register with `cli.Register(name, fn)` where `fn(w io.Writer, records []logread.Record, skipped int) error`.\n\n## Technical Decisions\n\n- Text is compared by byte value; CSV values are written without quoting.\n- A failing command writes nothing to standard output and one diagnostic line to standard error; stdout is buffered and flushed only on success.\n- Diagnostics (skip warnings, usage line, errors) go to standard error and are never compared.\n- No report answer, count or total is embedded in application code; every value comes from the log being read.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC arch-go-mod ===\nIntent: The root go.mod declares module ddkstat, Go 1.22 or newer, and no third-party requirement.\n\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\ntext = Path(\"go.mod\").read_text()\nlines = [ln.strip() for ln in text.splitlines()]\nassert any(ln.startswith(\"module \") for ln in lines), text\ngo_lines = [ln for ln in lines if ln.startswith(\"go \")]\nassert len(go_lines) == 1, text\nmajor, minor = go_lines[0].split()[1].split(\".\")[:2]\nassert (int(major), int(minor)) >= (1, 22), go_lines\nassert not any(ln.startswith(\"require\") for ln in lines), text\n=== END AC arch-go-mod ===\n\n=== AC arch-layout ===\nIntent: The directories every later story builds into exist.\n\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfor d in (\"cmd/ddkstat\", \"internal\", \"bin\"):\n    assert Path(d).is_dir(), d\n=== END AC arch-layout ===\n\n=== AC arch-no-vendor ===\nIntent: The module is standard-library only and has no go.sum dependency entries.\n\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nsum_path = Path(\"go.sum\")\nif sum_path.exists():\n    assert sum_path.read_text().strip() == \"\", sum_path.read_text()\nassert not Path(\"vendor\").exists()\n=== END AC arch-no-vendor ===\n\n## User Acceptance\n\n- None.\n\n## Guardrails\n\n- Standard library only; no third-party module and no database.\n- Only `cmd/ddkstat/main.go` calls `os.Exit`.\n- Application code never reads `sources/run.sh` and never embeds a report row, count or total.\n- The log being read is never written to.\n- Nothing under `sources/` is modified, regenerated or substituted.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-LogLines.md ===\n# FEATURE: Log Lines\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261026 V1 |\n| Description | Read the JSON Lines log into records, ignoring blank lines and classifying failed calls. |\n| Depends On  | ARCHITECTURE.md |\n| Provides    | logread.Read, logread.Record |\n| Consumes    | go.mod |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nTurn a JSON Lines stream into `logread.Record` values. Each non-blank UTF-8 line is one JSON object. This story establishes the reader, the `Record` type and the failed-call classification; later stories add skip rules, numeric normalization and model attribution.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- Read with `bufio.Reader` so no line-length limit applies.\n- A line that is empty after trimming whitespace is blank: ignored, not counted, but it still advances the 1-based physical line number.\n- Each object is decoded into a `Record` with `ExecutionID`, `StartedAt`, `Status`, `Command` (`job.command_name`), `Target` (`job.target`), `Provider` (`job.llm`), token counts, cost, `ElapsedMS`, `ReturnCode` (nullable) and `TimedOut`.\n- `Failed` is true when `Status` is not `succeeded`.\n- `Date` is the first 10 characters of `StartedAt`; `started_at` is accepted as any non-empty string with no further timestamp validation. A string shorter than 10 characters yields the whole string.\n\n## Required Go tests\n\nTestReadBlankLines, TestReadFailedStatus, TestReadStartDate, TestReadStartedAtNotValidated. Tests build their input as strings and call `logread.Read` with a `bytes.Buffer` warning writer.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC lines-blank ===\nIntent: Blank lines are ignored and not counted as records or skips.\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/logread\", \"TestReadBlankLines\")\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC lines-blank ===\n\n=== AC lines-failed-status ===\nIntent: A record whose status is not succeeded is classified as failed.\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/logread\", \"TestReadFailedStatus\")\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC lines-failed-status ===\n\n=== AC lines-start-date ===\nIntent: The record date is the first 10 characters of started_at.\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/logread\", \"TestReadStartDate\")\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC lines-start-date ===\n\n=== AC lines-started-at-lenient ===\nIntent: started_at is accepted as any non-empty string without timestamp validation.\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = lines-started-at-lenient ===\n\n## User Acceptance\n\n- None.\n\n## Guardrails\n\n- Never write to the log being read.\n- Blank lines are not counted anywhere but still count toward physical line numbers.\n- Standard library only.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-LogSkip.md ===\n# FEATURE: Log Skip\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261026 V1 |\n| Description | Skip malformed, incomplete and duplicate log lines with a stderr warning and no effect on the exit code. |\n| Depends On  | ARCHITECTURE.md, FEATURE-LogLines.md |\n| Provides    | logread.Read skip rules, logread.Result.Skipped |\n| Consumes    | logread.Read |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nMake the reader tolerant. Bad lines are skipped and counted; they never fail the run.\n\n## Behavior\n\n`logread.Read` returns the kept records and `Result.Skipped`, the whole-file count of skipped lines. A line is skipped when:\n\n- it is not a JSON object (invalid JSON, or valid JSON that is an array, string, number, boolean or null);\n- any of `execution_id`, `started_at`, `status`, `job.command_name`, `job.target` is absent or not a non-empty string;\n- its `execution_id` was already registered by an earlier kept line.\n\nFurther rules:\n\n- Each skipped line writes one warning to the supplied warning writer naming its 1-based physical line number, counting blank lines.\n- A skipped line does not register its `execution_id`; a malformed line therefore never causes a later line with that id to be skipped.\n- The first occurrence of an `execution_id` is kept.\n- Duplicate detection runs over the whole file inside `Read`, before any filter is applied elsewhere. A later repeat is skipped even when its first occurrence is later filtered out.\n- Skipping never changes the exit code; `Read` returns a non-nil error only for an I/O failure.\n\n## Required Go tests\n\nTestSkipNotObject, TestSkipMissingField, TestSkipDuplicateKeepsFirst, TestMalformedDoesNotRegisterID, TestSkipWarningLineNumber, TestReadDedupesWholeFile, TestSkipEmptyStringField.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC skip-not-object ===\nIntent: A line that is not a JSON object is skipped and counted.\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/logread\", \"TestSkipNotObject\")\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC skip-not-object ===\n\n=== AC skip-missing-field ===\nIntent: A line lacking a required field is skipped and counted.\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/logread\", \"TestSkipMissingField\")\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC skip-missing-field ===\n\n=== AC skip-empty-string-field ===\nIntent: A required field holding an empty or non-string value causes the line to be skipped.\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/logread\", \"TestSkipEmptyStringField\")\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC skip-empty-string-field ===\n\n=== AC skip-duplicate-first-kept ===\nIntent: A repeated execution_id is skipped and the first occurrence is kept.\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/logread\", \"TestSkipDuplicateKeepsFirst\")\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC skip-duplicate-first-kept ===\n\n=== AC skip-malformed-no-register ===\nIntent: A malformed line does not register its execution_id, so a later valid line with that id is kept.\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/logread\", \"TestMalformedDoesNotRegisterID\")\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC skip-malformed-no-register ===\n\n=== AC skip-warning-line-number ===\nIntent: Each warning names the 1-based physical line number, counting blank lines.\n\nfrom drydock_go result.stderr)\n=== END AC skip-dedupe-whole-file ===\n\n## User Acceptance\n\n- None.\n\n## Guardrails\n\n- Skipping never changes the exit code.\n- A malformed line never registers its `execution_id`.\n- Warnings go only to the warning writer (standard error in the CLI) and are never compared.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-LogNumbers.md ===\n# FEATURE: Log Numbers\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261026 V1 |\n| Description | Treat absent, null or non-numeric token, cost and duration values as zero. |\n| Depends On  | ARCHITECTURE.md, FEATURE-LogSkip.md |\n| Provides    | logread.Record numeric normalization |\n| Consumes    | logread.Read |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nAggregation sums these BEGIN ARTIFACT ARCHITECTURE.md ===\n# ARCHITECTURE: DrydockStats\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261026 V1 |\n| Description | Package layout, boundaries and pipeline of the ddkstat command-line reporter for Drydock's LLM execution log. |\n| Depends On  | None |\n| Provides    | go.mod, internal/, cmd/ddkstat/, bin/ |\n| Consumes    | None |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nddkstat reads a JSON Lines log (`logs/llm.jsonl`) and writes one of six CSV reports to standard output. It is a single-process, single-threaded CLI. It has no database, no network access and no configuration files. It never writes to the log it reads.\n\n## Technology Stack\n\nDerived from `TECHNOLOGY_STACK.md`:\n\n| Technology | Where it applies |\n|---|---|\n| Go 1.22 or newer, standard library only | All application code under `cmd/` and `internal/`. |\n| go test and go vet | Test runner behind `bin/test.sh`; tests live beside the code in `*_test.go`. |\n| JSON Lines | Input format, decoded with `encoding/json`, one object per physical line. |\n| POSIX sh | `bin/test.sh` and the supplied grader `sources/run.sh`. |\n\nNo third-party module is permitted. `go.mod` declares the module `ddkstat`, a `go` directive of 1.22 or newer, and no `require` lines.\n\n## Layout\n\n```text\ngo.mod                    module ddkstat\ncmd/ddkstat/main.go       package main; the only caller of os.Exit\ninternal/logread/         read, validate, dedupe and normalize log records\ninternal/filter/          pure record filters (target, date range)\ninternal/format/          minutes and cost text formatting\ninternal/report/          one aggregate-and-write function per report\ninternal/cli/             Run, argument parsing, report registry\nbin/test.sh               go vet ./... then go test ./...\nsources/                  read-only scoring assets; never touched by application code\n```\n\n## Module Ownership\n\n| Module | Owns | May access |\n|---|---|---|\n| `internal/logread` | Opening-agnostic reading of an `io.Reader`, line classification, skip and duplicate rules, numeric normalization, model attribution | `encoding/json`, `bufio`, `io`, `strings` |\n| `internal/filter` | Target and date-range predicates over `[]logread.Record` | `logread` types only |\n| `internal/format` | `Minutes` and `Cost` text | `fmt` |\n| `internal/report` | Aggregation and CSV writing for each report | `logread`, `format`, `io` |\n| `internal/cli` | Argument parsing, exit-code mapping, opening the log file read-only, report registry | `os`, `flag`, `time`, all internal packages |\n| `cmd/ddkstat` | Process start and `os.Exit` | `internal/cli` |\n\nPersistence, configuration files, file stores and external services: none. The log file is opened read-only by `internal/cli` and passed to `logread` as an `io.Reader`.\n\n## Pipeline\n\nParse arguments, read the log, drop skipped and duplicate lines, filter, aggregate, format. Parsing, aggregating and formatting are separate functions, each unit-tested.\n\n## Shared Types and Interfaces\n\n`logread.Read(r io.Reader, warn io.Writer) (Result, error)` returns `Result{Records []Record, Skipped int}`. The error is non-nil only when reading from `r` fails.\n\n`logread.Record` carries: `ExecutionID`, `StartedAt`, `Date` (first 10 characters of `StartedAt`), `Status`, `Failed`, `Command`, `Target`, `Provider`, `Model`, `InputTokens`, `CachedTokens`, `OutputTokens` (all `int64`), `CostUSD` (`float64`), `ElapsedMS` (`int64`), `ReturnCode` (`*int`, nil when null or absent) and `TimedOut` (`bool`).\n\n`cli.Run(args []string, stdout, stderr io.Writer) int` is the single entry point; exit codes are 0 success, 1 unreadable log, 2 usage error. Report writers register with `cli.Register(name, fn)` where `fn(w io.Writer, records []logread.Record, skipped int) error`.\n\n## Technical Decisions\n\n- Text is compared by byte value; CSV values are written without quoting.\n- A failing command writes nothing to standard output and one diagnostic line to standard error; stdout is buffered and flushed only on success.\n- Diagnostics (skip warnings, usage line, errors) go to standard error and are never compared.\n- No report answer, count or total is embedded in application code; every value comes from the log being read.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC arch-go-mod ===\nIntent: The root go.mod declares module ddkstat, Go 1.22 or newer, and no third-party requirement.\n\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\ntext = Path(\"go.mod\").read_text()\nlines = [ln.strip() for ln in text.splitlines()]\nassert any(ln.startswith(\"module \") for ln in lines), text\ngo_lines = [ln for ln in lines if ln.startswith(\"go \")]\nassert len(go_lines) == 1, text\nmajor, minor = go_lines[0].split()[1].split(\".\")[:2]\nassert (int(major), int(minor)) >= (1, 22), go_lines\nassert not any(ln.startswith(\"require\") for ln in lines), text\n=== END AC arch-go-mod ===\n\n=== AC arch-layout ===\nIntent: The directories every later story builds into exist.\n\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfor d in (\"cmd/ddkstat\", \"internal\", \"bin\"):\n    assert Path(d).is_dir(), d\n=== END AC arch-layout ===\n\n=== AC arch-no-vendor ===\nIntent: The module is standard-library only and has no go.sum dependency entries.\n\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nsum_path = Path(\"go.sum\")\nif sum_path.exists():\n    assert sum_path.read_text().strip() == \"\", sum_path.read_text()\nassert not Path(\"vendor\").exists()\n=== END AC arch-no-vendor ===\n\n## User Acceptance\n\n- None.\n\n## Guardrails\n\n- Standard library only; no third-party module and no database.\n- Only `cmd/ddkstat/main.go` calls `os.Exit`.\n- Application code never reads `sources/run.sh` and never embeds a report row, count or total.\n- The log being read is never written to.\n- Nothing under `sources/` is modified, regenerated or substituted.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-LogLines.md ===\n# FEATURE: Log Lines\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261026 V1 |\n| Description | Read the JSON Lines log into records, ignoring blank lines and classifying failed calls. |\n| Depends On  | ARCHITECTURE.md |\n| Provides    | logread.Read, logread.Record |\n| Consumes    | go.mod |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nTurn a JSON Lines stream into `logread.Record` values. Each non-blank UTF-8 line is one JSON object. This story establishes the reader, the `Record` type and the failed-call classification; later stories add skip rules, numeric normalization and model attribution.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- Read with `bufio.Reader` so no line-length limit applies.\n- A line that is empty after trimming whitespace is blank: ignored, not counted, but it still advances the 1-based physical line number.\n- Each object is decoded into a `Record` with `ExecutionID`, `StartedAt`, `Status`, `Command` (`job.command_name`), `Target` (`job.target`), `Provider` (`job.llm`), token counts, cost, `ElapsedMS`, `ReturnCode` (nullable) and `TimedOut`.\n- `Failed` is true when `Status` is not `succeeded`.\n- `Date` is the first 10 characters of `StartedAt`; `started_at` is accepted as any non-empty string with no further timestamp validation. A string shorter than 10 characters yields the whole string.\n\n## Required Go tests\n\nTestReadBlankLines, TestReadFailedStatus, TestReadStartDate, TestReadStartedAtNotValidated. Tests build their input as strings and call `logread.Read` with a `bytes.Buffer` warning writer.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC lines-blank ===\nIntent: Blank lines are ignored and not counted as records or skips.\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/logread\", \"TestReadBlankLines\")\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC lines-blank ===\n\n=== AC lines-failed-status ===\nIntent: A record whose status is not succeeded is classified as failed.\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/logread\", \"TestReadFailedStatus\")\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC lines-failed-status ===\n\n=== AC lines-start-date ===\nIntent: The record date is the first 10 characters of started_at.\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/logread\", \"TestReadStartDate\")\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC lines-start-date ===\n\n=== AC lines-started-at-lenient ===\nIntent: started_at is accepted as any non-empty string without timestamp validation.\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/logread\", \"TestReadStartedAtNotValidated\")\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC lines-started-at-lenient ===\n\n## User Acceptance\n\n- None.\n\n## Guardrails\n\n- Never write to the log being read.\n- Blank lines are not counted anywhere but still count toward physical line numbers.\n- Standard library only.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-LogSkip.md ===\n# FEATURE: Log Skip\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261026 V1 |\n| Description | Skip malformed, incomplete and duplicate log lines with a stderr warning and no effect on the exit code. |\n| Depends On  | ARCHITECTURE.md, FEATURE-LogLines.md |\n| Provides    | logread.Read skip rules, logread.Result.Skipped |\n| Consumes    | logread.Read |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nMake the reader tolerant. Bad lines are skipped and counted; they never fail the run.\n\n## Behavior\n\n`logread.Read` returns the kept records and `Result.Skipped`, the whole-file count of skipped lines. A line is skipped when:\n\n- it is not a JSON object (invalid JSON, or valid JSON that is an array, string, number, boolean or null);\n- any of `execution_id`, `started_at`, `status`, `job.command_name`, `job.target` is absent or not a non-empty string;\n- its `execution_id` was already registered by an earlier kept line.\n\nFurther rules:\n\n- Each skipped line writes one warning to the supplied warning writer naming its 1-based physical line number, counting blank lines.\n- A skipped line does not register its `execution_id`; a malformed line therefore never causes a later line with that id to be skipped.\n- The first occurrence of an `execution_id` is kept.\n- Duplicate detection runs over the whole file inside `Read`, before any filter is applied elsewhere. A later repeat is skipped even when its first occurrence is later filtered out.\n- Skipping never changes the exit code; `Read` returns a non-nil error only for an I/O failure.\n\n## Required Go tests\n\nTestSkipNotObject, TestSkipMissingField, TestSkipDuplicateKeepsFirst, TestMalformedDoesNotRegisterID, TestSkipWarningLineNumber, TestReadDedupesWholeFile, TestSkipEmptyStringField.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC skip-not-object ===\nIntent: A line that is not a JSON object is skipped and counted.\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/logread\", \"TestSkipNotObject\")\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC skip-not-object ===\n\n=== AC skip-missing-field ===\nIntent: A line lacking a required field is skipped and counted.\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/logread\", \"TestSkipMissingField\")\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC skip-missing-field ===\n\n=== AC skip-empty-string-field ===\nIntent: A required field holding an empty or non-string value causes the line to be skipped.\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/logread\", \"TestSkipEmptyStringField\")\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC skip-empty-string-field ===\n\n=== AC skip-duplicate-first-kept ===\nIntent: A repeated execution_id is skipped and the first occurrence is kept.\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/logread\", \"TestSkipDuplicateKeepsFirst\")\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC skip-duplicate-first-kept ===\n\n=== AC skip-malformed-no-register ===\nIntent: A malformed line does not register its execution_id, so a later valid line with that id is kept.\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/logread\", \"TestMalformedDoesNotRegisterID\")\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC skip-malformed-no-register ===\n\n=== AC skip-warning-line-number ===\nIntent: Each warning names the 1-based physical line number, counting blank lines.\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/logread\", \"TestSkipWarningLineNumber\")\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC skip-warning-line-number ===\n\n=== AC skip-dedupe-whole-file ===\nIntent: Duplicate detection covers the whole file and does not depend on any later filter.\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/logread\", \"TestReadDedupesWholeFile\")\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC skip-dedupe-whole-file ===\n\n## User Acceptance\n\n- None.\n\n## Guardrails\n\n- Skipping never changes the exit code.\n- A malformed line never registers its `execution_id`.\n- Warnings go only to the warning writer (standard error in the CLI) and are never compared.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-LogNumbers.md ===\n# FEATURE: Log Numbers\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261026 V1 |\n| Description | Treat absent, null or non-numeric token, cost and duration values as zero. |\n| Depends On  | ARCHITECTURE.md, FEATURE-LogSkip.md |\n| Provides    | logread.Record numeric normalization |\n| Consumes    | logread.Read |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nAggregation sums these ARTIFACT FEATURE-LogModel.md ===\n# FEATURE: Log Model\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261026 V1 |\n| Description | Attribute each record to the executed model, Purpose\n\nReports group by model, and the model that actually ran is more truthful than the one requested.\n\n## Behavior\n\n`Record.Model` is `result.stats.model` when that value is present and a non-empty string; otherwise it is `job.model`. This covers an absent value, an empty string, a non-string value, and a record with no `result.stats` object. `job.model` that is absent yields an empty `Model`; the record is still kept.\n\n##
